# Commons — differences between the four seasonal copies

Generated by `python -m lh.sources.com` from `build/lh.sqlite` (after `python -m lh build … COM`). LH-Communia.pdf prints every Common four times: Advent/Christmas (ADV, pp. 4–416), Lent (LENT, 417–817), Easter (EAS, 818–1207), Per annum (OT, 1208–1640). Elements are aligned by hour, slot, sub-hour, condition (`cond`: sub-variant, alternative) and order. An element that is the same object in every copy has collapsed and is not listed.

| | Elements |
|---|---|
| Aligned elements (Commons × hours × slots) | 1,634 |
| Identical in every copy (one object) | 606 |
| Differ only by *allelúia* (Easter copy; Lent openings without *Allelúia*) | 615 |
| Nearly identical: spelling, punctuation or a word (source discrepancy to review) | 24 |
| Different texts (seasonal hymns, antiphons, readings, Compline of Sunday …) | 171 |
| Printed in some copies only | 218 |

Kind: **alleluia** = the same words apart from *allelúia* (Easter short responsories also move the `*`); **variant** = nearly the same text (≥ 90 % similar) — see TODO.md §3 T-COM-2; **text** = different texts; **only in …** = the element exists only in the copies named. `= ADV` = the same object as printed in that copy. Pages are LH-Communia.pdf pages.
